← All modules

The zef ecosystem under Raku++

Every distribution in the REA index — latest release of each — fetched, built, installed and its own test suite run under Raku++.

637 of 2,524 distributions pass their own tests. A verdict names the first rung that failed, so a fix tends to move a dist one rung: pass passes its own test suite · build-fail its native build step fails · dep-build-fail a dependency’s build fails · dep-fail a dependency fails first · fetch-fail could not be fetched · other timeout / abnormal exit · self-fail its own suite fails · unresolved could not be resolved. The sweep, the fix campaign it drove, and the per-dist raw results live in the write-up; the trend is on the dashboard.
